Written f'(x) or (df)/(dx) in Leibniz's notation, ̇f(x) in Newton's notation (the latter used particularly when the independent variable is time)."],"raw_tags":["of a function of a single variable f(x)"],"topics":["calculus","mathematics","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The derivative of f(x)#61;x³ at x#61;2 is 12.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["One of the two fundamental objects of study in calculus (the other being integration), which quantifies the rate of change, tangency, and other qualities arising from the local behavior of a function.","The value of such a derived function for a given value of its independent variable: the rate of change of a function at a point in its domain."],"topics":["calculus","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["One of the two fundamental objects of study in calculus (the other being integration), which quantifies the rate of change, tangency, and other qualities arising from the local behavior of a function.","Any of several related generalizations of the derivative: the directional derivative, partial derivative, Fréchet derivative, functional derivative, etc."],"raw_tags":["of more general classes of functions"],"topics":["calculus","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["One of the two fundamental objects of study in calculus (the other being integration), which quantifies the rate of change, tangency, and other qualities arising from the local behavior of a function.","The linear operator that maps functions to their derived functions, usually written D; the simplest differential operator."],"qualifier":"generally","topics":["calculus","mathematics","sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/dɪˈɹɪvətɪv/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"something derived","word":"derivate"},{"sense":"something derived","word":"offshoot"},{"sense":"something derived","word":"spinoff"},{"topics":["linguistics","human-sciences","sciences"],"word":"derivate"},{"topics":["linguistics","human-sciences","sciences"],"word":"derived word"},{"topics":["finance","business"],"word":"contingent claim"},{"sense":"in analysis: function","word":"derived function"}],"upstream_line":12684,"upstream_offset":80798942,"upstream_record_sha256":"695ca6a77a488323b1433f92f1eabea8a3bb27a7b4e2e1441b20b484e1bdda2a","word":"derivative"}
